## Module 2 — Top-level admin panel (`docs/project-documentation/modules/module-2-admin-panel.md`)

Planned 2026-11-18 (M2-PLAN). UI spec: `docs/design/admin.md`. Order: backend before the screen that needs it, all `admin/` rows before the single `client/` row (M2-T13). Admin routes are TanStack Router file routes under `src/routes/_authed/admin/*`; every API call goes through TanStack Query at same-origin `/api/*`. New backend routes sit in `AdminModule` behind `SuperAdminGuard` (impersonated sessions → 403), with e2e in the M1-T2 style (`createAuthActors`, cleanup leaves the test DB empty). Rows marked **needs decision** need a product answer from the user before (or at the start of) that session; don't guess.

|---|---|---|---|

(boolean, default false) }` → 201 `{ message, ticket }`. `PATCH :id/status` `{ status }` → 200 `{ ticket, changed }`. 404 `Ticket not found` for unknown/malformed ids. Regression tests: unit (every key, 18 malformed tickets values, dashboard range/from/to) + component (`-tickets` 6 malformed URLs → only `skip=0&take=20`, one query key, no thread fetch, default filters/empty pane; `-clients` 3 URLs; `-dashboard` 3 URLs; `-login` non-string redirect), all red without the fix. **Flakes:** root cause = `autoCodeSplitting`: each route component is a lazy import Vite transforms on first render, so every file's first render (shell/login→dashboard/dashboard/system) took 0.4–0.6 s idle and > 1 s under load, past the 1 s `findBy*` default. Fix: `tanstackRouter({ autoCodeSplitting: !process.env.VITEST })` in `vite.config.ts` (components load eagerly at file import time, which has no timeout); dev/prod still split. No timeout raised, no retries. |

A first reply that finds the ticket IN_PROGRESS/RESOLVED changes nothing. `ticket.replied` + response shape unchanged. ## Module 3 — Client Auth, Impersonation Handoff & Meta (FB/IG) OAuth Connection (`docs/project-documentation/modules/module-3-client-meta.md`)

Planned 2026-11-18 (M3-PLAN). UI spec: `docs/design/client.md` (§1 language, §5 banner) + `docs/project-documentation/screens-inventory.md` C2/C3/C6. Order: backend before the screen that needs it (M3-T1…T7 backend, M3-T8…T11 `client/`). Builds on: Better Auth workspaces (`organization` plugin, sign-up creates the workspace), `WorkspaceAccessGuard` + our `@Roles` + `@CurrentWorkspace()` (M1-T1, pattern in `src/workspaces/workspaces.controller.ts`), `RedisModule`, env validation `src/config/env.validation.ts` (optional modules disabled when env unset, like `StorageModule`), client `_app` layout (`ssr: 'data-only'` guard, placeholder top bar with the EN/BN toggle), `useLanguage()`/`content(en, bn)`, `sessionQueryOptions`, and `/impersonate` (done in M2-T13). Conventions: Drizzle schema in `backend/src/db/schema/` (`id()`/`timestamps`, plural snake_case tables), `drizzle-kit generate` then review the SQL (never `push`); every query scoped by `workspace_id`; Graph API calls only through one mockable client (no live Meta calls in unit/e2e); e2e in the M1-T2 style (`createAuthActors`, cleanup leaves the test DB empty except the 4 `plans` rows); client: TanStack Query for every call at same-origin `/api/*`, shadcn OKLCH tokens, `rounded-full` buttons/pills/inputs, component tests mount the real file route under a bare root (M2-T13). Rows marked **needs user decision** must not start until the user answers; don't guess.

**Decisions (all answered 2026-11-18; each names the rows it affects):**
- **D1 Meta app credentials — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T3):** env `META_APP_ID`, `META_APP_SECRET`, `META_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID`, `META_OAUTH_REDIRECT_URI` (full callback URL, host per D6; all four together, client disabled when unset) · `appsecret_proof` on every token call + "Require App Secret" switched on in the app's Advanced settings · Meta **test app** (child of the prod app) for local + staging, live app for production; secrets only in git-ignored `backend/.env` / `backend/.env.staging` and the prod host's secret store. Still open (not blocking code): app owner/Business portfolio, App Review + Business Verification timeline (needed for the live check in GATE).
- **D2 Token encryption at rest — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T1):** env key `TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY` (base64 of 32 bytes) · versioned key id + keyring (`TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY_ID`, decrypt-only `TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_PREVIOUS_KEYS`) · one `text` column `v1:<keyId>:<iv>:<tag>:<ciphertext>` · AAD = workspace + purpose (+ subject id).
- **D3 Webhooks + local tunnelling — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** all webhook work is **Module 4**: M3-T7 moved to Module 4 (with D8's Deauthorize/Data Deletion); the M3-T6 toggle only flips `is_active` (`subscribed_apps` in Module 4). Defaults for Module 4: verify token from env, `X-Hub-Signature-256` checked in a route guard on the raw body, cloudflared tunnel.
- **D4 Permissions, login product, IG scope — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T2):** Facebook Login for Business (user-token `config_id` from env) · scopes `pages_show_list`, `pages_read_engagement`, `pages_manage_engagement`, `pages_read_user_content`, `pages_manage_metadata`, `business_management`, `instagram_basic`, `instagram_manage_comments` · IG via the Page's `instagram_business_account` (no Instagram Login) · exchange to the 60-day long-lived user token → non-expiring Page tokens · launch FB + IG · extra columns `social_pages.ig_username`, `meta_connections.granted_scopes text[]`.
- **D5 API shape, roles, impersonation — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** routes `/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/meta/oauth/url` + `/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/pages…` with `WorkspaceAccessGuard` + our `@Roles('owner','admin')` (member → 403) · impersonated sessions: **connect blocked (403)** so an admin's own Facebook account is never linked to a client, **page toggle allowed**. Only the OAuth callback lives outside the workspace prefix (`/api/meta/oauth/callback`, D6).
- **D6 OAuth redirect + state — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** redirect URI on the **client origin** `app.<domain>/api/meta/oauth/callback` via the same-origin proxy (session cookie rides along; the callback checks the state belongs to the same user + workspace) · state = random key in **Redis, 10-min TTL, single-use (GETDEL)** · callback lands back on the page that started it (`/onboarding` or `/pages`, allowlisted, stored in the state) with `?meta=connected` or `?meta=error&reason=…`.
- **D7 Connection cardinality — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T2):** one `meta_connections` row per workspace (unique `workspace_id`; re-connect updates it in place) · `social_pages.page_id` globally unique (+ `ig_business_id`): a Page already in another workspace is skipped with a reason, so each webhook maps to one workspace · Pages missing on re-connect: keep the row, `is_active = false`, Page token cleared · disconnect endpoint later (not Module 3).
- **D8 Token expiry/revocation — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T2):** `meta_connections.status` ACTIVE|NEEDS_REAUTH|REVOKED + `status_changed_at`; Graph error 190 → NEEDS_REAUTH (UI shows Reconnect) · `debug_token` job in Module 4 (BullMQ) · Deauthorize + Data Deletion callbacks in Module 4 with the webhook work (before App Review), so M3-T7 keeps only what D3 puts in Module 3.
- **D9 Graph API version — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T3):** pin `v26.0` (latest, released 2026-07-29) as `DEFAULT_GRAPH_API_VERSION` in `src/meta/meta-graph.config.ts`, optional env override `META_GRAPH_API_VERSION` (`^v\d+\.\d$`) · bump = change the constant, read the changelog for Pages/IG comment changes, run tests.
- **D10 Plan limit `connectedPages` — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** enforce **now** from `plans.feature_config.limits.connectedPages` (via `normalizeFeatureConfig`); a workspace with no plan gets the **Free** plan's limit · sync stores **every Page inactive** (user switches them on) · switching on past the limit is refused with **409** carrying the limit + current active count (UI shows an upgrade message).
- **D11 Impersonation banner — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** build it **now** (M3-T9) in the `_app` layout when `session.session.impersonatedBy` is set (en + bn); **Exit = sign out** the impersonated session, clear queries, then show "You can close this tab". Gate item 3 stays as written.
- **D12 Gate E2E harness — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** **committed Playwright suite** (`@playwright/test` dev dependency, own folder/project; the admin → client impersonation flow is its first spec), built in M3-T12. User also asked: install **`playwright-cli`** in the Docker image used to run it, and install its agent skill into this project folder with `playwright-cli install --skills`.
- **D13 Register + onboarding product rules — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** register fields = name, email, password + **business name** (used as the workspace name) · email verification + password reset **later** (no email sender yet) · users go to `/onboarding` after sign-up **and whenever the workspace has 0 active Pages**; it has a "Skip for now" link to `/dashboard`. · **0-Pages redirect: entry points only, answered 2026-11-18:** only after sign-in / sign-up without an explicit `?redirect=` (active workspace 0 active Pages → `/onboarding`, else `/dashboard`); `/dashboard` itself never redirects (so "Skip for now" needs no stored flag), `/impersonate` keeps landing on `/dashboard`, a failed pages check (network/5xx/403) → `/dashboard`.
- **D14 C6 boosted posts — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** **Module 6**. Module 3 `/pages` = base view only (list, status, toggle, reconnect, limit + needs-reauth messages); no `ads_read`, no Marketing API.
- **Implementation choices M3-T4…T6 — CONFIRMED by user 2026-11-18:** M3-T4: OAuth callback without a session → `?meta=error&reason=unauthenticated` (not straight to login); connect URL for a workspace that isn't the session's active one → 409; callback re-checks owner/admin + not impersonating. M3-T5: Pages skipped because they belong to another workspace are reported only as `?meta=connected&skipped=N` + a server log line; a Page whose IG account belongs to another workspace is left untouched; a Page missing on re-connect keeps its `page_id` (no other workspace can claim it until a disconnect endpoint exists, later module). M3-T6: pages list = `{ items, pageLimit: { limit, activeCount } }`; 409 `PAGE_LIMIT_REACHED` (`details.limit/activeCount`) + `PAGE_TOKEN_MISSING`; `AllExceptionsFilter` passes `code` + `details` through on 4xx (convention for all error bodies); Free plan limit = 1 Page, missing/malformed plan row → limit 0 (fail closed).

## Module 4 — Meta Webhook Ingestion & BullMQ Async Queue (`docs/project-documentation/modules/module-4-webhooks-queue.md`)

Planned 2026-11-18 (M4-PLAN). Spec goal: ingest live FB Page + IG comment webhooks with HMAC verification, a 200 OK in < 200 ms and idempotent BullMQ queuing; the worker de-duplicates by `comment_id` and stores `comments` rows as `PENDING` (Module 5 moderates them). Also carries Module 3's leftovers: M3-T7 (webhook verify + signature, Deauthorize + Data Deletion, tunnel; D3/D8), the `debug_token` job (D8), connection status + disconnect + `subscribed_apps` (D3/D7) and the two M3-GATE findings. Builds on: `MetaGraphClient` (M3-T3, the only code that calls Meta; extend it, e2e fakes it), `meta_connections`/`social_pages` (M3-T2, `page_id`/`ig_business_id` globally unique = one workspace per webhook), `META_APP_SECRET` env (M3-T3), Pages API + toggle tx (M3-T6, `src/meta/social-pages.*`), `RedisModule` (`REDIS_URL`), `AdminSystemService.queues()` stub (M2-T10; admin `QueuesCard` in `src/routes/_authed/admin/system.tsx` already renders `QueueStats[]`), client `/pages` + `components/meta/` + `lib/meta-pages.ts` (M3-T10/T11). Note: `main.ts` / `createNestApplication` use `bodyParser: false` (Better Auth), so the raw body for the HMAC check must be captured per route. Conventions as in Module 3: Drizzle schema in `backend/src/db/schema/` (`id()`/`timestamps`, plural snake_case tables, so the spec's `WebhookEvent`/`Comment` become `webhook_events`/`comments`), `drizzle-kit generate` then review the SQL (never `push`); every tenant query scoped by `workspace_id`; no live Meta calls in unit/e2e; e2e in the M1-T2 style (`createAuthActors`, cleanup leaves the test DB empty except the 4 `plans` rows; e2e Redis is stubbed, so queues need a test double or a real test Redis, see D3); errors may carry `code` + `details` (4xx); client: TanStack Query at same-origin `/api/*`, `content(en, bn)`, shadcn OKLCH tokens, `rounded-full` buttons/pills/inputs. All decisions answered 2026-11-18 (below); anything new that is a product call → ask, don't guess.

**Decisions (all answered 2026-11-18; each names the rows it affects):**
- **D1 Webhook routes + public host — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T3, M4-T4, M4-T13):** `GET` + `POST /api/webhooks/meta` (spec path under the global prefix) · Meta calls the **backend host directly** (not the client proxy; raw body untouched; locally via the D5 tunnel → `localhost:3000`) · missing/invalid `X-Hub-Signature-256` → **401** · verify token env `META_WEBHOOK_VERIFY_TOKEN` (optional like the other `META_*`; unset → 503).
- **D2 Webhook fields/topics — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T4, M4-T5, M4-T8):** Page `feed` (`item: comment`) + IG `comments`, **all verbs**: `add` (new row), `edited` (update message; scammers edit a bKash number in later), `remove` (mark deleted, never hide it later) · skip comments written by the Page/IG account itself · no `live_comments`/`mentions` · `subscribed_fields=feed` on `subscribed_apps`.
- **D3 Queues — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T1, M4-T4, M4-T5, M4-T7):** three queues `comment-ingest` (webhook → `comments` row), `comment-moderation` (Module 5; register now, no worker logic yet), `meta-token-check` (scheduled jobs) · 5 attempts, exponential backoff 2 s · `jobId` = `<platform>:<commentId>:<verb>` · `removeOnComplete` 1 h / 1000, `removeOnFail` 7 d / 1000 (Redis 30 MB) · workers in the API process for now · `@nestjs/bullmq` if it supports NestJS 12, else plain `bullmq` · e2e uses a fake queue provider (like the OAuth state store).
- **D4 Storage + routing — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T2, M4-T5, M4-T7):** `webhook_events.event_id` = `<platform>:<commentId>:<verb>` · comments for unknown / inactive Pages or REVOKED connections are **dropped** with a log line · `comments.status` only `PENDING` now (Module 5 adds values) · **only `raw_payload` is cleared after 30 days** (daily purge job; comment rows with text/author/status stay) · **lifetime analytics kept forever:** a daily aggregate table (per workspace + Page + platform + Dhaka day: received/edited/removed counts, Module 5 adds blocked etc.) updated by the worker in the same tx, never purged, no personal data · quota counting in Module 5/8.
- **D5 Local tunnel — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T13):** **zrok2** (installed in WSL + Windows, v2.0.8, account enabled) with the **static name `shield-comment-dev`** in the `public` namespace → `https://shield-comment-dev.shares.zrok.io` (name not reserved yet: the script creates it if missing). Repo script (e.g. `scripts/tunnel.sh`) remembers the name and runs `zrok2 share public localhost:3000 -n public:shield-comment-dev` (target port overridable), prints the webhook URL; agents may start/stop it when needed.
- **D6 Deauthorize + Data Deletion — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T6, M4-T12):** `POST /api/meta/deauthorize` + `POST /api/meta/data-deletion` (`@Public()`, `signed_request` verified) · Deauthorize: status REVOKED + all Pages switched off + tokens cleared · Data Deletion: queued job deletes the connection, its Pages and their comments (daily aggregates stay, no personal data); random confirmation code stored in `meta_data_deletion_requests`; status page on the **client** `app.<domain>/data-deletion?code=` (en/bn).
- **D7 Disconnect — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T9, M4-T11):** `DELETE /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/meta/connection`, owner/admin, **blocked while impersonated** (403, like connect) · hard delete `meta_connections` + `social_pages` rows (frees `page_id`/`ig_business_id` claims) · comments kept with their Page link set to null · best-effort `DELETE /{page-id}/subscribed_apps` per Page + `DELETE /me/permissions` (failures logged, never block). **Exception — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (user, M4-T9 choice 4, "Keep the exception (Recommended)"):** the app permissions are revoked only when the Facebook user has no other workspace still connected; the other workspace keeps working (built in M4-T9). The client dialog says the permission is revoked unless another ShieldComment workspace still uses that Facebook account (M4-T11).
- **D8 `debug_token` job — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T7):** daily at **03:00 Asia/Dhaka** · checks ACTIVE + NEEDS_REAUTH rows (a fixed token goes back to ACTIVE) · user token only · `is_valid=false` or a required D4-scope missing → NEEDS_REAUTH; REVOKED only from Deauthorize · no expiry warning yet (no email sender).
- **D9 Connection status + NEEDS_REAUTH — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T9, M4-T11):** `GET …/pages` adds `connection: { status, statusChangedAt, tokenExpiresAt } | null` · **soft block:** NEEDS_REAUTH → switching off allowed, switching on 409 `CONNECTION_NEEDS_REAUTH`, webhooks still ingested; REVOKED → no ingestion · Reconnect banner on `/pages` now (dashboard banner in Module 6).
- **D10 `subscribed_apps` on toggle — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T8):** Graph subscribe **before** the DB tx; failure → **502 `META_SUBSCRIBE_FAILED`**, nothing changes (UI rolls back); `token_invalid` → connection NEEDS_REAUTH · unsubscribe on switch-off is best effort (always switches off) · one-off backfill script for Pages already active from Module 3.
- **D11 Rate-limit IP fix — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T10):** **both**: the e2e seed / internal callers send `X-Forwarded-For`, and the backend falls back to the socket address when the header is missing, so nothing lands in `auth:no-trusted-ip|…`.
- **D12 Meta app owner — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T13):** a new **ShieldComment Business portfolio** owns the prod app + test app (user sets it up). **Skip the live Meta check for now**: build and gate with fakes only. **M4-T13 scope (user, answered 2026-11-19):** "Script + docs only (Recommended)": tunnel script + setup docs + live-smoke checklist; the live Meta smoke stays skipped until the test app exists (agents may create the zrok2 name and start/stop the share briefly).
- **D13 App Review + Business Verification — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** start **Business Verification now** (user, outside the code); submit **App Review after Module 5** (screencast can show hiding a scam comment; Data Deletion URL from M4-T6 + privacy policy needed).
- **Gate finding (a) — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T11):** impersonated `/pages` empty state hides the Connect CTA and shows the same impersonation notice as `/onboarding` (en/bn).
- **Repeated `edited` dedupe — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T4, M4-T5; from M4-T2 open a):** "Add payload hash (Recommended)": an `edited` job id / `event_id` carries a short hash of the new message, so exact retries are still ignored and every different edit is stored; `add`/`remove` keep the D3/D4 format. Built as `<platform>:<commentId>:edited-<16 hex>` (`commentEditHash()` = sha256 of the message, first 16 hex; still 3 colon parts for BullMQ), DB check relaxed in migration `0012_webhook_event_edit_hash`.
- **Out-of-order `edited`/`remove` — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T5 pre-check, user):** "Edit inserts, remove marks (Recommended)": `edited` with no comment row creates the comment from the payload as `PENDING` (a scam edit still gets moderated); `remove` with no row stores a deleted placeholder (no text), so a late `add` can't bring it back; counts move as for a normal edit/remove. A late `add` never un-deletes or overwrites (no-op).
- **Orphaned comments vs Data Deletion — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M4-T9; from M4-T6 open b, user via orchestrator):** "Keep FB user id on comments (Recommended)": comments keep D7's behaviour (kept on disconnect), but disconnect stamps each orphaned comment with the connection's `facebook_user_id`, so a later Data Deletion still finds and deletes them (+ their `webhook_events`). Daily counts stay. Built as nullable `comments.facebook_user_id` (numeric check, partial index, migration `0014_comments_facebook_user_id`).
- **Orchestration scope (user, 2026-11-18):** run M4-T1…M4-T12 then M4-GATE; M4-T13 (zrok script + live smoke) in its own session later.

IG: `feed` only (D2): IG `comments` come from the app's `instagram` webhook object, Meta only needs the app installed on the linked Page with any Page field (from Meta's docs, no live call). **Toggle** (`SocialPagesService.toggle`): **on** = pre-checks without locks or Graph (Page of this workspace else 404 · already on → no-op `changed:false`, **no Graph call** · no token → 409 `PAGE_TOKEN_MISSING` · limit → 409 `PAGE_LIMIT_REACHED`) → Meta or cipher unset → **503** (no Graph call) → decrypt (fails → 409 `PAGE_TOKEN_MISSING`, i.e. reconnect) → subscribe outside any tx → Graph failure → **502 `code: META_SUBSCRIBE_FAILED`**, nothing written (`token_invalid` also marks the connection) → the M3-T6 tx re-checks under locks; if it refuses (4xx, e.g. limit raced / Page gone) → best-effort unsubscribe, then the 4xx. **Off** = tx first (always), then `unsubscribeBestEffort` after commit (awaited, never throws; re-reads the Page scoped and skips it if gone / on again / no token / Meta unset; failures logged with ids + kind only, response unchanged). `PageSubscriptionService` (`src/meta/page-subscription.service.ts`) does decrypt + Graph + NEEDS_REAUTH for toggle + backfill; token queries in `page-subscription.queries.ts` (`findWorkspacePageToken`, `listActivePagesWithToken`, `markConnectionNeedsReauth` = one UPDATE: `ACTIVE` only (never over REVOKED, `status_changed_at` only on change) and only while the Page still holds the token that failed (re-connect wins)). `token_invalid` on unsubscribe also marks NEEDS_REAUTH. `AllExceptionsFilter`: 5xx keep `code` only if in `PUBLIC_SERVER_ERROR_CODES` (`META_SUBSCRIBE_FAILED`); `details` stay 4xx-only. **Backfill:** `backfillPageSubscriptions()` (`src/meta/page-subscription.backfill.ts`) + script `src/scripts/subscribe-active-pages.ts`: every active Page with a token (system-wide keyset read; NEEDS_REAUTH write scoped by `workspace_id` + Page row id), sequential, 200 ms apart, rate limit stops the run (rest `notAttempted`), prints counts only; Meta/cipher unset → "Skipped …" exit 0; exit 1 if any failed/undecryptable/not attempted. Run: host `cd backend && npm run build && npm run meta:subscribe-pages` · compose `docker compose exec backend node dist/scripts/subscribe-active-pages.js`. Idempotent (re-run any time). **Client:** `pageToggleFailure` → `{ kind: 'subscribe-failed' }` for 502 `META_SUBSCRIBE_FAILED` (en + bn message in `pageToggleErrorMessage`; optimistic rollback unchanged). Known small race (accepted): an unsubscribe after switch-off/refusal can land after a concurrent re-activation's subscribe; re-running the backfill repairs it. Tests: unit +45 (client calls, service, backfill, toggle order/502/503/compensation, filter); e2e +12 (`meta-page-subscribe.e2e-spec.ts`; `workspace-pages.e2e-spec.ts` now uses the strict fake `test/utils/fake-meta-graph.ts`); client +2 (502 rollback en + bn). |

## Module 5 — Bangla/Banglish AI Classifier & Meta Action Executor (`docs/project-documentation/modules/module-5-ai-moderation.md`)

Planned 2026-11-19 (M5-PLAN), decisions answered + rows re-planned 2026-11-19. Spec goal: classify every new or edited comment (Bangla script, Banglish, English, mixed) into `SAFE_INQUIRY` / `COMPETITOR_POACH` / `SCAM_PHISHING` / `SPAM_IRRELEVANT` / `ABUSIVE_TOXIC` with a structured LLM output `{ category, confidence_score, reasoning }`, then hide / delete / reply through the Meta Graph API per workspace policy. Gate: golden suite of 30+ real-world comments ≥ 90 % accuracy + mocked Graph tests for the exact hide/delete/reply payloads. Builds on: `AiGatewayService` (`backend/src/ai-gateway/ai-gateway.service.ts`: plain `fetch` to the OpenAI-compatible `/v1/chat/completions`, default model `gpt-oss-20b`, optional env `NEON_AI_GATEWAY_BASE_URL`/`NEON_AI_GATEWAY_TOKEN`/`AI_GATEWAY_MODEL`, `AiGatewayError{status, reason}`; no JSON mode, error kinds or retries yet; **live calls answer 403 `paid_plan_required` until AI credits are bought**), `QUEUE_NAMES.commentModeration` (`src/queue/queue.constants.ts`: registered, no worker; `DEFAULT_JOB_OPTIONS` 5 attempts exp 2 s; `commentJobId()` 3-part id rule; `withTimeout()` around queue calls), the hand-off `CommentIngestProcessor.handOffToModeration()` (`src/comments/comment-ingest.processor.ts`: after commit, `moderate: true` for created comments and applied edits of non-deleted ones; enqueues nothing yet), `comments` + `daily_comment_stats` (`src/db/schema/comments.ts`: `COMMENT_STATUSES = ['PENDING']` + check constraint; queries in `src/comments/comments.queries.ts`), `MetaGraphClient` (`src/meta/meta-graph.client.ts`: private `request(method, path, params, secrets)` = form body for POST, query for GET/DELETE, `appsecret_proof`; `MetaGraphError.kind` incl. `token_invalid`/`rate_limited`/`permission` + `retryable`, `src/meta/meta-graph.errors.ts`), the Page-token pattern of `PageSubscriptionService` + `markConnectionNeedsReauth` (`src/meta/page-subscription.*`: decrypt with `TokenCipherService` context `meta_page_token`, Graph outside the tx, `token_invalid` → NEEDS_REAUTH), granted scopes `pages_manage_engagement` + `instagram_manage_comments` (`META_LOGIN_SCOPES`), plans `feature_config` (`src/db/schema/plans.ts`, read via `normalizeFeatureConfig` in `src/admin/plan-rules.ts`: `limits.monthlyComments` free 500 / starter 2,500 / growth 15,000 / pro 75,000, which becomes `limits.monthlyAiCredits` in M5-T11 (D15); flags `banglaAiFilter`, `autoHideScams` (all plans), `autoReplyTemplates` (growth/pro), `customBlocklist`) + `workspaces.plan_id` (null = free plan, Module 3 D10 rule), admin stubs waiting for data (`AdminSystemService` for A6 "AI latency & token cost", dashboard `comments {scanned, blocked}` = 0, A3 Quota bar placeholder), scheduled-job pattern `MetaJobSchedulers` (`src/meta-callbacks/meta-scheduled.jobs.ts`), e2e doubles `test/utils/fake-queue.ts`, `fake-worker.ts` (processors tested via `app.get(X).process(job)`), `fake-meta-graph.ts` (strict: unexpected calls throw). Own comments/replies come back as webhooks but are already dropped by the envelope parser (`from.id === entry.id`, M4-T4). **Kept out (Module 6):** `ModerationRule` keywords/URL/BD-phone static rule engine that runs before the AI, `/comments` feed + manual override endpoint/UI (C5), `/rules` (C7), `/playground` (C8), dashboard/analytics (C4/C9). **AI credits (D15) are the only plan limit for moderation** (cost per AI call by the D8 context level; **SUPERSEDED 2026-11-19 by PW-D1/D2/D3**: monthly comments, counted once, in a per-account wallet, see section PW); **the AI provider, model and keys are chosen in `/admin/settings`** (D16; keys encrypted in the DB, `.env` fallback). **One `client/` row in Module 5:** the D8 "AI analysis" card (M5-T15); all other client moderation screens are Module 6 (D5/D10). Conventions as in Module 4: Drizzle schema in `backend/src/db/schema/`, `drizzle-kit generate` then review the SQL (never `push`); every tenant query scoped by `workspace_id`; **no live LLM or Meta calls in unit/e2e** (strict fakes that throw on unexpected calls; live checks are opt-in scripts); comment text is untrusted input (never logged, never followed as instructions; logs = ids/kinds/counts only); errors may carry `code` + `details` (4xx); admin: TanStack Query, shadcn OKLCH tokens. All decisions below were answered 2026-11-19; anything new that is a product call → ask, don't guess. Live AI rows (M5-T16) wait for the user's Neon AI credits or a provider key; M5-T17 waits for the Meta test app. **Re-planned 2026-11-20 (M5-AIPLAN):** Neon AI credits bought; Neon AI Gateway only in admin for now, a Neon model catalog file, a Text and a Text + Image model chosen in admin, a jev smoke first (D17–D23, rows M5-T15a…c before M5-T16).

**Decisions (all answered 2026-11-19; each names the rows it affects):**
- **D1 LLM provider, model + who pays — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T1, M5-T2, M5-T3, M5-T4, M5-T6, M5-T16):** **both** the Neon AI Gateway (with a model bake-off, M5-T16) **and** direct provider keys (OpenAI / Anthropic / Google); the active provider + model is chosen by the super admin in `/admin/settings` (D16) · ShieldComment pays (cost inside the plan prices; workspace use limited by D15 credits) · no live call until the user provides Neon AI credits or a provider key (live rows stop with `needs-decision`). **Narrowed 2026-11-20 by D17:** Neon AI Gateway only for now (credits bought); direct providers are hidden in admin, their backend code + `.env` fallback stay.
- **D2 AI failure behaviour — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T1, M5-T12):** fail open + sweeper: the comment stays visible with status `FAILED` + reason · transient errors (429, 5xx, timeout, network, one invalid JSON) use the D3 BullMQ retries · `not_configured` / `payment_required` / `auth` are not retried · a scheduled sweeper re-queues `PENDING`/`FAILED` comments younger than 24 h.
- **D3 Default action per category + mode — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T7, M5-T8, M5-T12):** auto mode on from the first comment, hide only: `SCAM_PHISHING`, `COMPETITOR_POACH`, `ABUSIVE_TOXIC`, `SPAM_IRRELEVANT` → hide; `SAFE_INQUIRY` → nothing · delete is never a default (a workspace may opt in per category through the D5 API).
- **D4 Confidence threshold — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T7, M5-T8, M5-T12):** one per-workspace threshold, default **0.80** (Module 6's `auto_hide_threshold` reuses it): ≥ threshold → the D3 action; below → no action, status `NEEDS_REVIEW` (visible; Module 6's feed lists it).
- **D5 Policy storage + where it is edited — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T7, M5-T8, M5-T15):** `moderation_policies` (one row per workspace, created lazily with the D3/D4/D6/D8 defaults: mode, action per category, threshold, auto-reply switches + en/bn texts, AI context level) + `GET/PATCH /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/moderation/policy` (owner/admin, impersonation rule like the Page toggle) · Module 6 builds `/rules` (C7) on it · Module 5's only client UI = the D8 "AI analysis" card (M5-T15). · **User wish (2026-11-19, golden review of abuse-01):** a client page listing each comment category with a description + an example comment, where the client picks Show / Hide / Delete per category; kept in **Module 6** (`/rules`, C7) on this API.
- **D6 Auto-reply — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T5, M5-T7, M5-T8, M5-T9, M5-T12):** off by default · only on plans with `autoReplyTemplates` (growth/pro) · per-category opt-in with one en + one bn text · the classifier returns `language` (`bn`/`banglish`/`en`) → bn text for bn/banglish, en otherwise · at most one auto-reply per comment, none to deleted comments · `SAFE_INQUIRY` only when the owner switches it on.
- **D7 Credit use — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T11, M5-T12, M5-T13) — SUPERSEDED 2026-11-19 by PW-D1/D2/D3** (each comment counts 1 once, before its first AI call; edits free; out of comments → `SKIPPED comment_limit_reached`; built in PW-T4). Old text: credits (D15) are used on **every AI call**: the first classification and every re-classified edit · reserved atomically before the call (the balance never goes below 0; cost by the D8 level) · out of credits → no AI call, status `SKIPPED` reason `credits_exhausted`, comment stays visible, one warn line per workspace per day · no email yet (alerts = Module 7). Replaces the planned comment-count quota.
- **D8 Data sent to the LLM — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T5, M5-T7, M5-T8, M5-T10, M5-T11, M5-T12, M5-T15)** (credit costs per level superseded 2026-11-19 by PW-D1: every level counts 1 comment; the levels stay): chosen **by the client per workspace** (owner/admin; stored in the D5 policy), 3 levels: **"Comment only"** (default, 1 credit: comment text trimmed ≤ 1,000 chars + platform + is-a-reply) · **"+ Page name"** (1 credit; better at competitor poaching) · **"+ post caption"** (2 credits; best context; caption from Graph, cached per post, M5-T10) · never the author name/id; phone numbers and links kept · the client "AI analysis" card shows a one-line pro/con per option in en + bn (user: more data sent = more credits but a better filter; comment-only analyses just the text).
- **D9 `reasoning` retention — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T5, M5-T7):** short English reasoning (≤ 280 chars) kept on the comment like its text (removed by Data Deletion with the comment) · raw LLM responses never stored.
- **D10 Human override scope — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T7, M5-T9):** Module 5 builds executor methods for hide / unhide / delete / reply + a `moderation_actions` log (actor = system or user id), no HTTP override endpoint · Module 6 adds `POST /comments/:id/override` + C5 · a human action stamps the comment so the AI never changes it again.
- **D11 Edited comments — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T8, M5-T9, M5-T12):** **re-check + auto-unhide** (the user's choice, not the recommended option): every applied edit is re-classified (and charged, D7); the AI may escalate (visible → hidden) and **un-hides a comment it hid itself when the edit is safe** · a human-stamped comment (D10) is never changed by the AI.
- **D12 Golden suite + live checks — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T6, M5-T16, M5-GATE):** agent-drafted fixture of ≥ 40 anonymised comments (Bangla script, Banglish, English, mixed; ≥ 6 per category; injection attempts, bKash/Nagad numbers, wa.me links, BD phone formats), **labels reviewed by the user** (M5-T6 pauses for it) · CI replays recorded responses (no paid calls) · accuracy = opt-in live `npm run ai:golden` (M5-T16) · gate (1) = that live run. **Label review done 2026-11-19:** all labels approved except emoji-only comments: positive/neutral reactions → `SAFE_INQUIRY`, negative/insulting → `ABUSIVE_TOXIC` (prompt `moderation-v2` teaches it); **if the M5-T16 live run shows the model can't tell emoji sentiment reliably, the fallback is to hide all emoji-only comments.**
- **D13 Which comments are moderated — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T12):** every stored comment with text on connected Pages, FB + IG, organic + ad posts, top-level + replies · skipped: deleted, no text, orphaned (`social_page_id` null), Page switched off · connection NEEDS_REAUTH → classified, Meta action skipped (`connection_needs_reauth`).
- **D14 Admin visibility — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T13, M5-T14):** A6 `/admin/system` "AI moderation" card (today + 7 d: classifications, tokens, estimated cost USD + BDT, avg/max latency, failures by kind; per provider + model) · A2 dashboard `comments {scanned, blocked}` from `daily_comment_stats` · A3 clients **credits** bar (`used / limit` credits this month) instead of the quota bar. **Credits bar part SUPERSEDED 2026-11-19 by PW-D1/D3:** the A3 bar shows monthly comments `used / limit · resets <date>` from the wallet (PW-T6b, PW-T7); the AI card loses its credits figure.
- **D15 Credits (new, replaces the comment-count limit) — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T11, M5-T12, M5-T13, M5-T14) — SUPERSEDED 2026-11-19 by PW-D1/D2/D3** (limit = `limits.monthlyComments` again, per client account, reset on the billing date, no Free plan; credits code and tables removed in PW-T4/PW-T6b). Old text: each plan gets N **AI credits per month** (`feature_config.limits.monthlyAiCredits`, seeded from today's `monthlyComments` numbers 500 / 2,500 / 15,000 / 75,000, editable in the `/admin/settings` plans editor, plan audit logged) · **credits are the only limit**: `limits.monthlyComments` stops being a limit; "up to ≈ N comments" is display text only (an estimate, not checked) · cost per AI call by the D8 level: comment only 1, + Page name 1, + post caption 2 · reset each Asia/Dhaka calendar month, no rollover · no plan = free plan · top-up packs later (Module 8).
- **D16 AI provider settings (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T1, M5-T2, M5-T3, M5-T4, M5-T6, M5-T16):** the super admin picks the active provider (**Neon AI gateway / OpenAI / Anthropic / Google**) + model in `/admin/settings` · API keys stored **encrypted in the DB** (`TokenCipherService`), `.env` values as fallback · keys never shown again after saving (masked) · "Test connection" button · every change in an audit log (keys only as set/cleared) · super admin only. **Provider choice narrowed 2026-11-20 by D17/D22:** the admin card shows Neon only (no provider select, no direct-provider keys) and picks a Text model + a Text + Image model from the D19 catalog; the API keeps the provider/key fields.
- **D17 Neon AI Gateway only (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20 (M5-T15a, M5-T15b, M5-T15c, M5-T16):** user: "I have added $5 ai credit to my neon data base … you can proceed the test with neon database only." and "For now Ignore the direct provider method disable in the admin panel or don't show it." → every live run uses the Neon AI Gateway (credentials `NEON_AI_GATEWAY_BASE_URL` + `NEON_AI_GATEWAY_TOKEN` in git-ignored `backend/.env.neon`; never printed) · admin hides the direct providers · backend keeps the direct-provider adapters, API fields and `.env` fallback (smaller than removing; reversible). Orchestrator check 2026-11-20 (`GET /v1/models`, free): 65 models, all enabled.
- **D18 Candidate models + cost rule (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20 (M5-T15a, M5-T15b, M5-T16):** user: "From there select those models … gemini-4-carbon, typesafeai-jev-1-14-0, claude-haiku-5-5, gpt-oss-20b, gpt-oss-120b" and "try to select the cheapest model" → the M5-T16 bake-off runs these five on Neon; winner = **cheapest passing ≥ 90 %** (D12). Spend cap for all live calls: the US$2 cap of 2026-11-19 still applies (stop and ask before exceeding it). · M5-T15a (2026-11-20): jev is served only on Neon's `systemone` dialect (typed questions, not chat), so it is judged through a System One adapter (D24); its output is billed $0 (input-only pricing, Neon Decisions doc), cost = input tokens × $0.042 / 1M.
- **D19 Model catalog file (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20 (M5-T15b, M5-T15c):** user: "Store the info to our backend a constant file or json file, like model name, model code, lab, supported input type, context window, input price, output price." Source https://neon.com/ai-gateway#models (page "Last updated 2026-11-17", USD per 1M tokens, "no Neon markup") + live `/v1/models` `input_modalities`. Facts collected 2026-11-20 (M5-T15b re-checks the page):
  | Model code | Name | Lab | Inputs (live `/v1/models`) | Context | Input $/1M | Output $/1M |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | `gemini-4-carbon` | Gemini 4 Carbon | Google | text, image, video, audio, file | 1M | "Free" | "Free" |
  | `typesafeai-jev-1-14-0` | Jev 1.14 | TypeSafe AI | text, image (page: "Decision models", no input types shown) | 66K | 0.042 | not shown |
  | `claude-haiku-5-5` | Claude Haiku 5.5 | Anthropic | text, image, file | 200K | 0.10 | 0.50 |
  | `gpt-oss-20b` | GPT OSS 20B | OpenAI | text | 131K | 0.07 | 0.30 |
  | `gpt-oss-120b` | GPT OSS 120B | OpenAI | text | 131K | 0.15 | 0.60 |
  "Free" stays visible as free (price 0 + a `free` flag) and "not shown" as unknown (`null`, cost "n/a"); no invented numbers.
- **D20 Gateway setup (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20 (M5-T15b):** user: "First setup the neon ai gateway. Doc ref - https://neon.com/docs/ai-gateway/get-started." The backend already calls Neon through `OpenAiCompatibleAdapter` (`POST {base}/v1/chat/completions`, `Authorization: Bearer <token>`, `max_tokens`, `response_format`); M5-T15b checks it against the doc and fixes or lists gaps.
- **D21 jev smoke first (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20 (M5-T15a):** user: "I want you to do some test first in bangla, english, banglish with the typesafeai-jev-1-14-0." → opt-in live script only, a handful of golden comments per language, tiny spend, nothing recorded for CI.
- **D22 Model per content type (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20 (M5-T15b, M5-T15c, M5-T16):** user: "Can you make the admin to choose the model for type of content to be judge with. like For Text & Image -> claude-haiku-5-5 For text -> typesafeai-jev-1-14-0. Give the dropdown option to choose by the admin. make sure if for text + image only the supported models are listed. include the pricing details to that drop down and context window also." → two settings, **Text model** (default `typesafeai-jev-1-14-0`) and **Text + Image model** (default `claude-haiku-5-5`, image-capable models only), dropdowns from the D19 catalog showing lab, input/output price per 1M and context window; defaults stay until the M5-T16 bake-off picks winners. · M5-T15a: the default Text model (jev) needs the `systemone` path (D24), so M5-T15b wires the live classifier by model dialect (chat vs systemone).
- **D23 What "Text + Image" judges today (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20 (M5-T15b, M5-T16):** today no image reaches the classifier: `CommentClassifier` sends text only, the webhook envelope keeps only FB `message` / IG `text`, and photo/sticker-only comments end `SKIPPED` `no_text` (D13); FB keeps the change `value` in `comments.raw_payload` (purged after 30 days), where Meta's feed webhook may carry a `photo` URL. Options: (a) **store the Text + Image setting now, classifier stays text-only; image moderation (FB comment photo → image-capable model, prompt `moderation-v3`, image golden items) becomes a later row** (recommended: matches today's data, smallest change) · (b) add an image row now (M5-T15d: read the photo URL, send it to the Text + Image model, golden image items) before M5-T16 · (c) drop the Text + Image dropdown until images are supported. · **Answer (user, 2026-11-20):** "Save setting, images later and show a enable disable switch button with it." → (a) plus an on/off switch: store the Text + Image model and an enabled flag now; the classifier stays text-only; image moderation is a later row.
- **D24 jev through a System One adapter (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20 (M5-T15a, M5-T15b, M5-T16):** Neon serves `typesafeai-jev-1-14-0` only at `POST {base}/typesafe/v1/systemone` (`/v1/models`: `dialects: ["systemone"]`, chat 400 "not available on the chat_completions endpoint"). User: "Build a jev adapter." (+ project-scope plugin `typesafe@typesafe-ai`, skill `typesafe-ai`). User also pasted Neon's client example (`@typesafe-ai/sdk` `TypeSafeClient({ apiKey: NEON_AI_GATEWAY_TOKEN, baseURL: `${NEON_AI_GATEWAY_BASE_URL}/typesafe`, defaultModel })` → `client.systemOne({ state, questions })`) → use the official SDK, and listed the other Neon dialect docs (OpenAI Responses, Anthropic Messages, Gemini) for reference. Built in M5-T15a: `src/ai-gateway/systemone.adapter.ts` (SDK 0.7.0 pinned, retries / logs / zstd off, errors → the shared `AiErrorKind`s) + `src/moderation/jev-moderation.ts` (5 Nouls, category decided in code, prompt version `jev-v1`); wired only into the golden script. M5-T15b wires the live classifier by model dialect.
- **D25 AI analysis log (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20 (M5-T15d, M5-T15e):** user: "Can you show the comment analyzed logs in my admin panel. for now show only last 20 analyzed comments, what have decision it made (like hide, block, allowed), how much it cost, comment context, for which client, (store the input token and output token amount (not the price store the context length like 0.4k)) Check if possible to show the remaining credit to my dashboard, total credit we have used so far, how many comments are analyzed, etc log i want to see it in dashboard if possible." → admin `GET /api/admin/ai/analyses?limit=20` (max 20): newest analyzed comments across all clients (time, client + Page, comment text (admin-only view, never logged), category + confidence, action NONE = Allowed / HIDE = Hidden / DELETE = Deleted + failed / skipped states as stored, model, input + output tokens as counts (UI formats `0.4k`), estimated cost USD = tokens × catalog prices (jev output 0, unknown price → `null` "n/a"), latency), from the existing token/usage columns (`backend/src/db/schema/moderation.ts`; new columns only if missing, with a migration); shown on the admin dashboard (M5-T15e).
- **D26 Credit top-ups + spend dashboard (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20 (M5-T15d, M5-T15e):** Neon has no API for the AI Gateway credit balance or dollar spend (Console → Billing only; exhaustion = HTTP 429 `credits_required`). User: "Admin will enter topup info with amount and topup date." → new table of credit top-ups (amount USD > 0, top-up date, optional note, created by, created at) + admin `GET`/`POST` (+ audited `DELETE` for a mistaken entry); `GET /api/admin/ai/summary`: total credit added, estimated spend to date (all stored calls), estimated remaining = added − spend, comments analyzed (total, today, last 7 days), tokens in / out, spend by model and by decision. Dashboard cards say "Estimate from our token counts; exact balance: Neon Console → Billing".
- **Module 6 carry-over (user, 2026-11-19):** `/rules` = per-category settings page: each category (`SAFE_INQUIRY` … `ABUSIVE_TOXIC`) shown with a short description + an example comment, and a Show / Hide / Delete choice per category (stored through the D5 policy API).

## Plans, subscriptions & wallet (PW) (plan: `docs/plans/2026-11-19-plans-subscriptions-wallet.md`)

Planned 2026-11-19 (PW-PLAN), all decisions answered 2026-11-19. Goal: **replace Module 5's AI credits with a monthly comment limit**, give every client (billing) account **one wallet** with metered limits that reset monthly on the billing date, add **subscriptions** with a **Starter trial at sign-up**, **monthly + yearly** intervals and **no Free plan**, pool plan limits (comments, Pages, seats, workspaces) across the account's workspaces, and let the super admin **activate a plan manually**. Runs **before M5-T16** (the Module 5 run resumes at M5-T16 → M5-GATE after PW-GATE). It **pulls the subscription part of Module 8 forward**; payments (SSLCommerz), renewals, trial → paid conversion, upgrades/downgrades, the landing pricing cards and `/billing` **stay in Module 8**. Full design (data model, service APIs, migration order, out of scope): the plan doc §3. Conventions as in Module 5: Drizzle schema in `backend/src/db/schema/`, `drizzle-kit generate` + reviewed SQL (never `push`, never edit 0000–0020); every tenant query scoped by `workspace_id` (billing queries also by account id); text enums + check constraints; FKs restrict (e2e `cleanup()` + `e2e/` teardown delete children first); logs = ids/counts only; 4xx bodies may carry `code` + `details`; admin/client: TanStack Query, shadcn OKLCH tokens, client copy `content(en, bn)`. **Apps:** admin/ + client/ approved for every PW row (user, 2026-11-19).

**Decisions (all answered 2026-11-19; verbatim answers in `docs/progress/ORCHESTRATION.md` "Decisions asked", PW lines):**
- **PW-D1 Limit model (PW-T1b, PW-T2, PW-T4):** drop AI credits entirely → **monthly comments**: each comment counts 1, at any D8 context level.
- **PW-D2 Edits (PW-T4):** a comment counts **once**; AI re-checks after edits are free.
- **PW-D3 Wallet (PW-T1, PW-T2):** **one wallet per client (billing) account**, multi-meter (`monthlyComments` now; auto-reply, lead capture later); structure ready for **top-ups / extra limit for a month** (`wallet_balances.added`, ledger kind `TOP_UP`), **none built now**.
- **PW-D4 Reset (PW-T2, PW-T3):** monthly **on the billing date** (same day + time of day as the start, clamped to the month end; Asia/Dhaka); yearly plans also reset monthly; the reset time is **stored** (`wallets.current_period_end`) and shown.
- **PW-D5 Interval (PW-T1, PW-T1b, PW-T6, PW-T7):** **monthly + yearly**; yearly = an **exact per-month price per plan** set by the admin (e.g. ৳500 monthly vs ৳450/month yearly = ৳5,400 charged); the card shows the monthly figure.
- **PW-D6 Launch offer (PW-T1b, PW-T6):** **keep** the struck list price + badge next to the monthly/yearly prices. **SUPERSEDED 2026-11-20 by PR-D8 + PR-D4:** offer fields leave the plan; today's offers become seeded "Launch offer" DISCOUNT promotions (PR planner's reading 4).
- **PW-D7 Free plan (PW-T1b, PW-T5):** **no Free plan**: the `free` row is deactivated (`is_active = false`) and kept for audit history; the "no plan = Free" fallback goes away.
- **PW-D8 Trial (PW-T1, PW-T1b, PW-T3, PW-T7):** **Starter trial at sign-up**, once per account, no payment; length **admin-adjustable** (presets 3 / 7 / 14 / 30 days; seed 7); a change applies to **new trials only** (started trials keep their stored end). **SUPERSEDED 2026-11-20 by PR-D10:** no auto trial at sign-up; the client starts a trial from a TRIAL promotion; the adjustable length moves to the promotion's `trial_days`.
- **PW-D9 Expiry (PW-T2, PW-T3, PW-T4, PW-T8):** trial/term ends unpaid → **moderation pauses** (comments stay visible, `SKIPPED no_active_plan`; Pages stay connected; login works; "Choose a plan" banner); nothing deleted.
- **PW-D10 Workspace limit (PW-T1b, PW-T5):** plans get `limits.workspaces`, seed **1 / 3 / 10** (Starter / Growth / Pro).
- **PW-D11 Pooling (PW-T5, PW-T6):** limits are a **shared pool per client account** across its workspaces (comments, Pages, seats, workspaces).
- **PW-D12 Trial allowance (PW-T1, PW-T1b, PW-T3):** trial comment limit **300**, admin-editable (`plans.trial_comment_limit`, Starter only); other trial limits = Starter's. Replaces the draft default "full Starter limits". **SUPERSEDED 2026-11-20 by PR-D10:** the trial comment limit moves to the TRIAL promotion (`trial_comment_limit`); the plan column is dropped in PR-T7.
- **PW-D13 Yearly seed (PW-T1b):** yearly per-month prices **10% off**, rounded down to whole taka: Starter ৳449, Growth ৳1,349, Pro ৳3,599 (editable). **SUPERSEDED 2026-11-20 by PR-D8:** yearly per-month list prices are recomputed (৳949 / ৳2,349 / ৳5,599) so that, with the seeded Launch offer, the shown prices stay ৳449 / ৳1,349 / ৳3,599 (PR planner's reading 4).
- **PW-D14 Limit edits (PW-T2):** a changed plan limit applies **from each client's next reset** (the wallet stores the per-period grant); prices are snapshotted on the subscription.
- **PW-D15 Existing data (PW-T3):** the migration gives each existing client account a **fresh Starter trial**.
- **PW-D16 Manual activation (PW-T9):** **admin "activate plan manually"** now: the super admin picks plan + monthly/yearly + start date for a client; audit-logged.
- **Planner's readings (PW-PLAN) — CONFIRMED 2026-11-19 (user):** (trial parts SUPERSEDED 2026-11-20 by PR-D10: trials come from TRIAL promotions, not Starter's plan columns) a trial is **one wallet period** `[start, trial end)` granted the trial limit at trial start (so a trial-limit change applies to new trials only, like `trial_days`) · trial plan = slug `starter`; trial fields accepted only on Starter; `trial_days = null` = no trial · no live plan → can't create workspaces (403 `no_active_plan`) and can't switch Pages **on** (409 `NO_ACTIVE_PLAN`; off always works) · `workspaces.billing_account_id` stays nullable in the DB (a member-less workspace has no account = no live plan) · client summary route = `/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/billing/summary` (repo tenant-route convention) · manual activation: start date from 31 days ago to today (Dhaka), no future dates, reason required (1–500), ends the live subscription (CANCELED) and starts a fresh wallet period with `used` 0 on the chosen start date.

## Promotions (PR) (plan: `docs/plans/2026-11-20-promotions.md`)

Planned 2026-11-20 (PR-PLAN), all decisions answered 2026-11-20. Goal: **plans hold only their fixed facts** (prices, limits, features, an optional badge + description in en + bn, one highlighted plan) and **every offer is a separate, stoppable promotion** of kind **TRIAL**, **DISCOUNT** or **COUPON** that targets one, several or all plans, with an end date (or "until stopped"), an optional "first N clients" cap and a once-per-client-account claim. The trial no longer starts at sign-up: the client picks a plan on a new client `/plans` page. Runs **after PW-GATE and before M5-T16** (the Module 5 run resumes at M5-T16 → M5-GATE after PR-GATE). **Checkout, payments, renewals and invoices stay in Module 8**; Module 8 adds the public "apply coupon" endpoint and the invoice promotion lines (plan doc §5.5, §5.6, recorded in the Module 8 spec). Full design (tables, price rule, APIs, screens): the plan doc §5. Conventions as in PW: Drizzle schema in `backend/src/db/schema/`, `drizzle-kit generate` + reviewed SQL (never `push`, never edit 0000–0026); text enums + check constraints; FKs restrict (e2e `cleanup()` + `e2e/` teardown delete children first); billing queries scoped by account id, tenant queries by `workspace_id`; logs = ids/counts only; 4xx bodies may carry `code` + `details`; admin/client: TanStack Query, shadcn OKLCH tokens, client copy `content(en, bn)`. **Apps:** admin/ + client/ approved for every PR row (user, 2026-11-20: "Both, whole PR run (Recommended)").

**Decisions (all answered 2026-11-20; verbatim answers in `docs/progress/ORCHESTRATION.md` "Decisions asked", PR lines; table in the plan doc §2):**
- **PR-D1 Name (all rows):** umbrella term **Promotion**, kinds **TRIAL / DISCOUNT / COUPON**.
- **PR-D2 Shared settings (PR-T1, PR-T2, PR-T4, PR-T5b):** every promotion has **target plans** (one / several / all), **validity** (an end date, or until the admin turns it off), an optional **cap "first N clients"** and an admin **Stop** at any time.
- **PR-D3 Once per client (PR-T1, PR-T2):** "Make sure offer claims for each unique user only one time. Next time they will not get those offers." → **once per client (billing) account per promotion**, ever (`unique(promotion_id, billing_account_id)`).
- **PR-D4 Discount types (PR-T1, PR-T2):** **FLAT** (৳ off) or **PERCENT** (% off); the admin chooses **total bill** or **each month's price**.
- **PR-D5 Discount length (PR-T1, PR-T2):** the admin sets the **month count** ("first 3 month he will get 10% off") or **forever**.
- **PR-D6 No stacking (PR-T2):** when automatic discounts overlap, **only the bigger saving applies**.
- **PR-D7 Percent label (PR-T2, PR-T6):** a percentage shows as **plain text** ("50% off"), **not a badge**.
- **PR-D8 Plan badge + description (PR-T1, PR-T4, PR-T5, PR-T6, PR-T7):** plans get an optional free-text **badge** ("Popular") + a short **description**; the launch-offer fields (struck original price, offer price, offer badge) **leave the plan**.
- **PR-D9 Highlighted plan (PR-T1, PR-T4, PR-T5, PR-T6):** the admin marks **one** plan as highlighted: stronger card style + **primary** button; the others get **secondary** buttons.
- **PR-D10 Trial = promotion (PR-T3, PR-T6):** targeted plans show **"Start free N-day trial"**; the client picks; **no auto trial at sign-up**; after the end date the trial **is hidden** and later sign-ups **get no trial**.
- **PR-D11 Coupon (PR-T2, PR-T3b, Module 8):** the code is **typed at checkout** and **validated by the backend**; never shown on cards.
- **PR-D12 Bill shows the offer (PR-T1, PR-T3b, Module 8):** every bill records the promotion: name/code, amount off and, for multi-month discounts, "k of N".
- **PR-D13 Buy button now (PR-T6):** "Choose plan" = **"Contact us"** until Module 8 checkout.
- **PR-D14 Pricing layout (PR-T6):** like the user's reference: **Monthly/Yearly switch** with "Save up to N%", cards (name, optional badge, description, discounted price with the normal price struck + "50% off" / "৳200 off" text, button, feature list).
- **PR-D15 Stop / end date (PR-T2, PR-T3b):** "Keep what they got (Recommended)": Stop or the end date blocks **new claims only**; claimed discounts run their months (forever = until the client cancels or leaves the targeted plans).
- **PR-D16 Coupon + discount (PR-T2, PR-T3b):** "Coupon on top (Recommended)": the coupon comes off the **already discounted** price; automatic discounts still don't stack; floor **৳0**.
- **PR-D17 Bangla plan text (PR-T1, PR-T4, PR-T5, PR-T6):** "Yes, en + bn fields (Recommended)": `badge` and `description` in en + bn; bn falls back to en when empty.
- **PR-D18 Contact us target (PR-T1, PR-T2, PR-T4, PR-T5, PR-T6):** "WhatsApp chat": "Contact us" opens a WhatsApp chat (prefilled message naming the plan + interval); the number is set in admin settings; **hidden when unset**.
- **PR-D19 Coupon months (Module 8; user, 2026-11-20):** "For coupon it is one time use mean if i set for first 3 months, it will apply only to that first three month, but usually it will be applied only to one month and one time only." A coupon is redeemed **once per client account**; it covers **only the months the admin set** (first N bills from redemption, `months_used` + 1 per term, then stops), and the **usual length is 1 month** (first bill only).
- **Planner's readings (approved with the plan 2026-11-20, user: "yes please proceed"; plan doc §3):** (1) **one trial per client account, ever**, across all trial promotions; discounts/coupons once per promotion · (2) yearly + "first N months": a per-month discount comes off N of the 12 months; a "total bill" discount ignores the month count and applies to the first bill only · (3) a percentage price is **rounded down** to whole taka; a flat discount never goes below ৳0 · (4) **today's card prices stay identical**: list price → `monthly_price_bdt`; per plan with an offer a seeded **"Launch offer"** DISCOUNT (FLAT, each month, that plan, forever, no end date, value = list − offer: ৳500 / ৳1,000 / ৳2,000); yearly per-month list = today's yearly + that value (৳949 / ৳2,349 / ৳5,599); seeded TRIAL **"Starter 7-day trial"** (Starter, 7 days, 300 comments, no end date) · (5) sign-up creates the account + first workspace but **no plan** and lands on client **`/plans`**; the "choose a plan" banner links there · (6) existing subscriptions untouched (trials keep end + limit, ACTIVE keeps its price snapshot) · (7) **manual activation (PW-T9) takes an optional coupon code** (the only coupon path before Module 8) and applies + records the best live discount · (8) after a promotion's first redemption its **money terms are locked** (type, value, scope, months, trial days, trial comment limit); name, end date, cap, targets and Stop stay editable; redemptions snapshot their terms · (9) a claimed discount **belongs to the client account** and carries across terms (`months_used`) until its months run out (forever: until the client moves to a plan it doesn't target); signed-in clients see their own price on `/plans`, visitors the best live discount · (10) "bigger saving" compares **the first bill** (what the card shows).

## Module 6 — Client Dashboard Charts, Live Feed, Rules Engine & Playground (`docs/project-documentation/modules/module-6-client-dashboard.md`)

Planned 2026-11-20 (M6-PLAN). UI spec: `docs/design/client.md` §5 + `docs/project-documentation/screens-inventory.md` C4, C5, C7, C8, C9 (and C6 boosted posts, Module 3 D14). Spec goal: a static rule engine (blocked keywords, URLs, BD phone `(?:\+88)?01[3-9]\d{8}`) that runs **before** the AI classifier and skips the LLM call on a match; client screens `/dashboard` (KPIs + Recharts Line, Bar, Donut), `/comments` (live feed + manual Hide / Unhide / Delete / Reply), `/rules`, `/playground`, `/analytics` (+ CSV). Gate: rule-priority unit test (keyword / BD-phone match never calls the LLM) + e2e UI test (dashboard charts render; "Unhide" on `/comments` changes the comment status). Order: backend rows (M6-T1…T7) before the client screen that needs them (M6-T8…T12). Builds on: `comments` (status `COMMENT_STATUSES`, latest classification columns, `status_reason` machine codes, `overridden_at`, `moderation_stage`) + `daily_comment_stats` (Dhaka day × workspace × Meta `page_id` × platform, counters `DAILY_COMMENT_COUNTERS`: received/edited/removed/classified/5 categories/hidden/unhidden/deleted/replied/skipped/failed; written by ingest + the moderation worker only, so they are **AUTO** outcomes) in `src/db/schema/comments.ts`; `moderation_actions` (AUTO|USER log) + `moderation_policies` (mode, `confidence_threshold` 0.800, `ai_context_level`, per-category action + auto-reply + en/bn texts) in `src/db/schema/moderation.ts`; `GET/PATCH /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/moderation/policy` (`ModerationPolicyService`, owner/admin, impersonated ok; 409 `PLAN_FEATURE_UNAVAILABLE`); `CommentModerationService.moderate()` (tx1 skips + `WalletService.consume(monthlyComments)` → policy + post context → `classify()` → tx2 `decideAction` → executor → tx3); `ModerationActionExecutor.execute({ source: 'USER', userId })` (hide/unhide/delete/reply, USER stamps `overridden_at`, skip reasons, `fakeCommentActionGraph()` for e2e); plan flags `customBlocklist` (Growth/Pro), `autoReplyTemplates` (Growth/Pro), `whiteLabelReports` (Pro) via `workspacePlanFeatures()`; admin dashboard helpers `src/admin/dashboard-range.ts` (Dhaka buckets) + chart pattern (admin M2-T6: Recharts, `var(--token)` colours, no shadcn `chart`); client `_app` shell, `mockApi` harness, `lib/moderation-policy.ts` (policy mirror, key `['workspace',id,'moderation','policy']`). Client has **no `recharts`** and **no app navigation** yet (top bar only; `/dashboard` is the SW-T7 placeholder). Workspace roles: owner / admin / moderator.

**Reconciling the spec with Modules 4/5 (planner's readings, no decision needed unless you disagree):** (1) spec `ModerationRule` = **extend `moderation_policies`** (no new table): `auto_hide_threshold` = the existing `confidence_threshold` (M5 D4), `auto_reply_templates` = the existing per-category `<cat>AutoReply/ReplyEn/ReplyBn` (M5 D6, see D14), new columns only for blocked keywords / URL / phone rules. (2) spec `DailyAnalytics` = **`daily_comment_stats`** (+ one new counter `ruleMatched`). (3) spec `GET/POST/PATCH /rules` = the existing **`GET/PATCH …/moderation/policy`**, extended with `rules` (no separate `/rules` route, no POST). (4) spec `POST /comments/:id/override` = `POST /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/comments/:commentId/override` on `ModerationActionExecutor` (M5 D10). (5) every route is under `/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/…` behind `WorkspaceAccessGuard` + our `@Roles`, scoped by `workspace_id`. (6) user overrides are not in the daily counters (AUTO only); the feed shows them from `moderation_actions` + `overridden_at`. (7) `/rules` (C7) holds the Module 6 carry-over (per-category description + example + Show / Hide / Delete = policy action `NONE` / `HIDE` / `DELETE`), descriptions + made-up examples as static en + bn client copy.

**Decisions (all answered 2026-11-20; each names the rows it affects; options listed recommended first):**
- **D1 Live feed transport (M6-T3, M6-T9) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) polling every 15 s while the tab is visible + "N new comments" pill (`since` param).** (a) **polling**: TanStack Query `refetchInterval` 15 s while the tab is visible + a "N new comments" pill (`since` param), no new infra (recommended) · (b) Server-Sent Events from the worker's tx3 via Redis pub/sub · (c) Neon Realtime (provisioned, not wired).
- **D2 What a rule match does (M6-T1, M6-T2) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (b) the client picks Hide or Delete per rule kind (keyword / URL / phone, default Hide, stored in the policy `rules.actions`); the rest of (a) stays: REVIEW mode → `NEEDS_REVIEW`, no AI category stored, `status_reason` `rule_keyword`/`rule_url`/`rule_phone` + the matched value kept on the comment, `ruleMatched` counter.** (a) AUTO mode → **Hide** (REVIEW mode → `NEEDS_REVIEW`), no AI category stored, `status_reason` `rule_keyword` / `rule_url` / `rule_phone` + the matched value kept on the comment, counted in `ruleMatched` (recommended) · (b) the client picks Hide or Delete per rule kind · (c) a match maps to a category (keyword → `SPAM_IRRELEVANT`, URL/phone → `SCAM_PHISHING`) and follows that category's action.
- **D3 Rule matches and the monthly comment limit (M6-T2) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) a rule match counts 1 like any moderated comment; no plan / limit reached → `SKIPPED`.** (a) a rule-matched comment **counts 1** like any moderated comment (PW-D1 "each comment counts 1"), and limit reached / no plan → `SKIPPED` as today (recommended) · (b) rule matches are free but still paused without a live plan · (c) free and run even over the limit.
- **D4 Plan gating of rules (M6-T1, M6-T10) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) URL + BD-phone rules on every plan (`autoHideScams`), custom keywords only with `customBlocklist` (Growth/Pro).** **Runtime too (user, 2026-11-20):** "Saved keyword rules will not keep working after a client moves to a smaller plan if that plan doesn't offer that feature." → the worker and the playground run only the rules the live plan allows (`rulesForPlan`: keywords need `customBlocklist`, URL + phone `autoHideScams`; no live plan → none); saved rules stay stored and come back on upgrade (replaces M6-T2 choice 4). (a) URL + BD-phone blocks on **every plan** (`autoHideScams`), custom keywords only with `customBlocklist` (Growth/Pro) (recommended) · (b) every rule needs `customBlocklist` · (c) no gating.
- **D5 Keyword match rules (M6-T1, M6-T2) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) case-insensitive substring after NFKC + lower-case + whitespace collapse, phrases allowed, Bangla as typed, ≤ 200 keywords × 1–50 chars, de-duplicated.** (a) **case-insensitive substring** after NFKC + lower-case + whitespace collapse; phrases allowed; Bangla script matched as typed (no transliteration, a Banglish spelling is its own keyword); up to **200 keywords × 1–50 chars** per workspace, duplicates removed (recommended) · (b) whole-word only (Bangla word boundaries are unreliable) · (c) also allow regex (power users, ReDoS risk).
- **D6 URL + phone rule defaults and exceptions (M6-T1, M6-T2) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) both off by default; own-domain allow-list (facebook.com / instagram.com / m.me always allowed); phone also matches +880, dashes, Bangla digits.** buyers often type **their own number** to order. (a) both **off by default**, the client switches them on; URL rule has an allow-list of the client's own domains (+ facebook.com / instagram.com / m.me always allowed); phone also matches `+880 17…` / dashes / Bangla digits ০–৯ (recommended) · (b) both on by default (with the allow-list) · (c) phone only when the comment also has bKash/Nagad/payment words.
- **D7 Who may use the feed and override (M6-T3, M6-T4, M6-T9) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) every member (owner, admin, moderator) may use the feed and act; impersonated sessions may act, logged with the session user.** (a) feed + Hide / Unhide / Delete / Reply for **every member** (owner, admin, moderator); impersonated sessions may act (support), the action is logged with the session user (recommended) · (b) owner/admin act, moderators read-only until Module 7 · (c) like (a) but impersonated = read-only.
- **D8 Review-queue actions (M6-T4, M6-T9) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) add "Keep visible" (USER stamp, `VISIBLE`, no Graph call); Reply free text 1–500 chars.** `NEEDS_REVIEW` comments are visible and waiting. (a) add **"Keep visible"** (USER stamp `overridden_at`, status `VISIBLE`, no Graph call) next to the four spec actions; Reply = free text 1–500 chars (recommended) · (b) only Hide / Unhide / Delete / Reply · (c) also "Re-check with AI" (costs a call).
- **D9 Playground cost + limit (M6-T7, M6-T11) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: user: "Free 10/day. And make sure admin can configure this from its setting page." → free, default 10 tests per workspace per Dhaka day; the limit is a platform setting the super admin edits on `/admin/settings` (row M6-T7b).** a test runs rules then the AI (jev ≈ $0.00003 per call), stores nothing, never calls Meta. (a) **free, 20 tests per workspace per Dhaka day** (recommended) · (b) each test counts 1 against monthly comments · (c) free, no cap · (d) rules only, no AI.
- **D10 Dashboard KPIs + ranges (M6-T5, M6-T8) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) 7d / 30d / 90d (default 30d), 4 KPIs + Line / Bar / Donut.** (a) ranges **7d / 30d / 90d** (default 30d, Dhaka days, zero-filled); KPIs: comments scanned (classified + rule-matched), auto-hidden, needs review now (live count), scams blocked; Line = scanned vs auto-hidden per day, Bar = flagged categories (non-SAFE), Donut = all five intents (recommended) · (b) same plus a Page filter · (c) only today + 7 days.
- **D11 Analytics scope ("Agency") (M6-T6, M6-T12) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) per workspace + Page filter, up to 12 months (daily ≤ 92 days, else monthly).** (a) **per workspace** (+ Page filter), up to 12 months (daily ≤ 92 days, else monthly); a cross-workspace view waits for a workspace switcher (recommended) · (b) account-wide roll-up across the billing account's workspaces for `whiteLabelReports` (Pro) · (c) both.
- **D12 CSV export (M6-T6, M6-T12) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (b) daily aggregates CSV (≤ 366 days, every plan, no comment text) plus a per-comment CSV (time, Page, platform, text, status, category, confidence, language, last action; no author ids), ≤ 92 days, Pro only (`whiteLabelReports`).** (a) **daily aggregates** (day, Page, platform, every counter) for the chosen range ≤ 366 days, every plan, no comment text (recommended) · (b) also a per-comment CSV (time, Page, platform, text, status, category, confidence, language, last action; no author ids) ≤ 92 days, Pro only (`whiteLabelReports`) · (c) both for every plan.
- **D13 PDF reports (screens inventory C9 "CSV/PDF") (M6-T12) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) not in Module 6.** (a) **out of Module 6** (later, with white-label reports) (recommended) · (b) a print stylesheet (browser "Save as PDF") · (c) server-made PDF.
- **D14 Auto-reply templates (M6-T1, M6-T10) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) keep M5 D6 (one en + bn text per category, Growth/Pro, edited on `/rules`).** (a) **keep M5 D6**: one en + bn text per category, switched on per category (Growth/Pro), edited on `/rules` (recommended) · (b) several texts per category, rotated · (c) keyword-triggered replies (e.g. "price" → a price text).
- **D15 C6 boosted posts (Module 3 D14 said Module 6) (M6-T13) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) not in Module 6 → M6-T13 moved to the backlog (until Meta app review for `ads_read`).** needs `ads_read` + the Marketing API (new scope, Meta app review). (a) **out of Module 6** → backlog until the Meta app passes review (recommended) · (b) in: read-only list of active boosted posts per Page on `/pages` · (c) in, with a per-post moderation switch.
- **D16 Client app navigation (M6-T8) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (c) bottom bar on mobile + sidebar on desktop.** (a) a **sidebar** like admin's (`sidebar.tsx` floating panel, sheet on mobile): Dashboard, Comments, Pages, Rules, Playground, Analytics, Plans (recommended) · (b) tab links in the top bar · (c) bottom bar on mobile + sidebar on desktop.
- **D17 How the gate's e2e UI test avoids live Meta (M6-T4, M6-GATE) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) test-only `META_GRAPH_BASE_URL` (refused in production) + a small Graph stub in `e2e/`; Playwright clicks Unhide on a seeded HIDDEN comment.** "Unhide" calls the Graph API, and `MetaGraphClient` has a fixed `https://graph.facebook.com` host. (a) optional env `META_GRAPH_BASE_URL` (test/compose only, refused in production) + a tiny Graph stub server started by the root `e2e/` suite; Playwright on the compose stack clicks Unhide on a seeded HIDDEN comment (recommended) · (b) Playwright on the compose stack with the comment's Page token missing → only checks the error path; the status change is proven by the backend e2e + client component test · (c) gate (2) = client component test (mocked API) + backend e2e, no Playwright.
