## Module 7 — Client Support Ticket Portal, Team RBAC & Alerts (`docs/project-documentation/modules/module-7-support-team.md`)

Planned 2026-11-20 (M7-PLAN). UI spec: `docs/project-documentation/screens-inventory.md` C11 (`/settings/team`), C12 (`/settings/alerts`), C13 (`/support`), A5 (`/admin/tickets`, admin side); `docs/design/client.md` has no settings/support section (only the pricing matrix rows "Team / Moderator Seats 1 | 3 | 10" and "WhatsApp & Telegram Alerts ✗ | ✓ | ✓", and the impersonation banner), so the screens follow the Module 6 client patterns. Spec goal: client ticket endpoints on the shared `SupportTicketsModule` (`GET/POST /support/tickets`, `POST /support/tickets/:id/reply`); an `AlertSettings` table + a spike dispatcher queue when the hourly scam count exceeds `spike_threshold` (Email / Telegram / WhatsApp); workspace invites + member roles through the Better Auth `organization` plugin with seat limits from the plan and invitation emails through a transactional email provider; client screens C11, C12, C13 with `content(en, bn)`. Gate: round-trip e2e (client creates a ticket on `/support` → it appears on admin `/admin/tickets` → admin replies → client sees the reply) + a mocked alert test that fires when the scam threshold is exceeded. Order: backend rows (M7-T1…T9) before the admin row (M7-T10) and the client screens that need them (M7-T11…T15). **Builds on:** `SupportTicketsModule` (`src/support-tickets/`: `tickets` + `ticket_messages` in `src/db/schema/support-tickets.ts`, categories `BILLING`/`TECHNICAL`/`ACCOUNT`/`OTHER`, priorities `LOW`/`MEDIUM`/`HIGH`, statuses `OPEN`/`IN_PROGRESS`/`RESOLVED`, `senderRole` `ADMIN`/`CLIENT`, internal notes admin-only, subject 1–200, body 1–5,000; `SupportTicketsService.list/detail/addMessage/updateStatus/findItem`; `statusAfterReply()` (client reply reopens RESOLVED, first public admin reply OPEN → IN_PROGRESS); `TicketEvents.onReplied()` in-process emitter "for later notifications"; admin routes only `GET/POST/PATCH /api/admin/tickets…`, M2-T7/T14 decisions) + admin A5 `admin/src/routes/_authed/admin/tickets.tsx` + `admin/src/lib/admin-tickets.ts` (no polling, no attachments); `StorageService` (`src/storage/`, Neon Object Storage over S3, presigned URLs, optional env → `StorageNotConfiguredError`, used only by `neon-smoke` so far); Better Auth `organization` plugin in `src/auth/better-auth.ts` (`creatorRole: 'owner'`, models `workspace` / `workspaceMember` / `workspaceInvitation`, hooks only `beforeCreateOrganization` + `afterCreateOrganization`; **no `sendInvitationEmail`, no invitation expiry setting** (plugin default 48 h)) + roles in `src/auth/permissions.ts` (owner = plugin owner, admin = plugin admin, moderator = plugin member statements); `WorkspaceAccessGuard` + `@Roles` (`src/auth/guards`, `src/auth/decorators/roles.decorator.ts`); sign-up always creates a first workspace + billing account + trial (`src/auth/first-workspace.ts`); **no email provider anywhere** (no Resend / nodemailer / SMTP, no email verification or password reset mail); `CLIENT_APP_URL` env (links into the client app); plan `limits.teamSeats` 1 | 1 | 3 | 10 (Free inactive | Starter | Growth | Pro) and `features.messengerAlerts` (Growth/Pro) in `src/db/schema/plans.ts`; `LimitService.seatsLeft(accountId)` + pure `seatsLeftOf()` (`src/billing/limit.service.ts`, PW-D11: live plan `teamSeats` − distinct members across the account's workspaces, owner included, no live plan → 0; "not enforced yet"); `workspacePlanFeatures()` (`src/moderation/moderation-policy.service.ts`); BullMQ queues `commentIngest` / `commentModeration` / `metaTokenCheck` / `billing` (`src/queue/queue.constants.ts`, `queue.registry.ts`, `job-schedulers.ts` with `Asia/Dhaka` crons); `comments` (index `(workspace_id, created_at desc)`, `category`, `status_reason` `rule_keyword`/`rule_url`/`rule_phone`) + `daily_comment_stats.scamPhishingCount` (per Dhaka **day** only); M6-GATE root `e2e/` Playwright suite on the compose stack (+ test-only `META_GRAPH_BASE_URL` stub pattern); client `_app` shell + `src/components/app-shell/app-nav.tsx` (7 items: Dashboard, Comments, Pages, Rules, Playground, Analytics, Plans; bottom bar < `md`), `mockApi` test harness, no `/settings/*`, `/support` or `/invite` routes, no workspace switcher.

**Reconciling the spec with what exists (planner's readings, no decision needed unless you disagree):** (1) the spec's client ticket routes become **`/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/support/tickets`** (`GET` list, `POST` create, `GET :ticketId` thread, `POST :ticketId/reply`) behind `WorkspaceAccessGuard` + `@Roles`, every query scoped by `workspace_id`; they **reuse the M2 tables and `SupportTicketsService`** (`addMessage()` with `senderRole: 'CLIENT'`, `statusAfterReply()`); internal notes are never returned to the client; no new ticket tables except attachments (D4). (2) A client-created ticket shows up on `/admin/tickets` through the existing admin list (no admin change for the round trip; admin changes only for D3/D4). (3) The client's "new reply" marker = the last public message is from `ADMIN` (no read-tracking table). (4) Anti-abuse: at most 20 unresolved tickets per workspace (409 `TICKET_LIMIT_REACHED`); subject/body limits stay the M2 ones. (5) Team writes use the **Better Auth organization endpoints** (`organization/invite-member`, `accept-invitation`, `cancel-invitation`, `update-member-role`, `remove-member`) called from the client with the Better Auth React client inside TanStack Query mutations, enforced by server hooks (`beforeCreateInvitation`, `beforeAcceptInvitation`, `beforeUpdateMemberRole`, `beforeRemoveMember`, `sendInvitationEmail`): no wrapper write routes. One read route **`GET /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/team`** (members with name/email/role/joined, pending invitations, account seats used/limit) because seats are pooled per billing account (PW-D11) and need `LimitService`. (6) A removed member loses access on the next request (the guard checks membership); their sessions are not revoked. (7) `AlertSettings` = new table **`alert_settings`** (one row per workspace; no row = alerts off, default threshold) in a new `src/alerts/` module, plus a delivery log **`alert_deliveries`** (workspace, channel, status, scam count, error kind, sent at; no message text) used for the cool-down audit and "last alert" on C12. (8) The hourly scam count comes from **`comments`** (index `(workspace_id, created_at)`): `category = 'SCAM_PHISHING'` **or** `status_reason in ('rule_url', 'rule_phone')` (rule matches store no category, M6 D2); `daily_comment_stats` is per day and can't give an hour. (9) New BullMQ queue **`alerts`** in `QUEUE_NAMES` (D3 retry defaults); an alert enqueue failure is logged and never fails moderation. (10) Emails and alert messages are bilingual (English, then Bangla) and carry no comment text and no author ids; links go to `CLIENT_APP_URL`. (11) Alerts are gated at run time like M6 D4: only channels the live plan allows are sent; saved settings stay and come back on upgrade. (12) Alert settings: owner/admin edit, moderators read; a "Send test alert" button (max 5 per workspace per hour) uses the real channels.

**Decisions (all answered 2026-11-20; verbatim answers in `docs/progress/ORCHESTRATION.md` "Decisions added by orchestrator"; each names the rows it affects; options listed recommended first):**
- **D1 Who may open and see tickets (M7-T2, M7-T12) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) every member opens tickets; owner + admin see all of the workspace's, a moderator sees own.** (a) every member may open a ticket; owner + admin see all of the workspace's tickets, a moderator sees only their own (billing tickets stay private) (recommended) · (b) every member sees every workspace ticket (shared inbox) · (c) only owner + admin use support.
- **D2 Impersonated sessions in Module 7 (M7-T2, M7-T5, M7-T6, M7-T7, M7-T12…T15) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) impersonated sessions read everything and may edit alert settings; no tickets, replies or team changes (403 `IMPERSONATION_READ_ONLY`).** During impersonation the session user *is* the client, so a ticket or reply would look like the client wrote it. (a) impersonated sessions may **read** tickets, team and alerts and may edit alert settings, but may not create tickets/replies or change the team (403 `IMPERSONATION_READ_ONLY`; the screens show why) (recommended) · (b) read-only everywhere in Module 7 · (c) full access (like M6 D7), actions logged with the session user.
- **D3 Ticket form, priority and the status the client sees (M7-T2, M7-T10, M7-T12) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) client picks category, subject, message; support sets priority, Pro (`prioritySupportApi`) defaults `HIGH`; client can't change status; a reply reopens a resolved ticket.** (a) the client picks category (the 4 M2 categories) + subject + message; priority is set by support only (default `MEDIUM`, **`HIGH` by default for workspaces whose live plan has `prioritySupportApi`** (Pro), shown as a badge in the admin queue); the client sees Open / In progress / Resolved, can't change the status, and a reply on a resolved ticket reopens it (M2 rule) (recommended) · (b) same, without the Pro priority · (c) the client also picks the priority and may mark a ticket resolved.
- **D4 Screenshot attachments (C13 "attach screenshot") (M7-T3, M7-T10, M7-T12) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (b) up to 3 images per message (PNG/JPEG/WebP, ≤ 5 MB each) for client AND admin messages, via `StorageService`, shown through presigned URLs.** (a) one optional image per client message (PNG/JPEG/WebP, ≤ 5 MB) stored with `StorageService` (Neon Object Storage), table `ticket_attachments`, shown through short-lived presigned URLs on `/support` and `/admin/tickets`; when storage isn't configured the upload is hidden with a note (recommended) · (b) up to 3 images per message, admin replies too · (c) no attachments in Module 7.
- **D5 Telling the client about admin replies (M7-T4, M7-T12) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) polling + "New reply" badge on `/support`, plus an email to the requester.** (a) in-app: `/support` polls the open thread every 15 s and the list every 60 s while the tab is visible (M6 D1 style) + a "New reply" badge on the ticket and on the Support nav item; **plus an email** to the ticket's requester on each public admin reply (via `TicketEvents.onReplied`, needs D6) (recommended) · (b) in-app only · (c) email only.
- **D6 Transactional email provider (M7-T1, M7-T4, M7-T5, M7-T9) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) Resend HTTP API via fetch; dev logs the email; tests use a fake (built in M7-T1).** Nothing is wired today. (a) **Resend** HTTP API via `fetch` (no SDK; env `RESEND_API_KEY`, `EMAIL_FROM`; free tier 3,000/month, 100/day; needs a verified sending domain); without a key the backend logs the email (dev) and tests use a fake that fails on unexpected sends (recommended) · (b) SMTP via `nodemailer` (any provider: Zoho, Brevo, Gmail app password) · (c) Brevo HTTP API (free 300/day).
- **D7 Invites: who may invite which role, and link expiry (M7-T5, M7-T13) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) owner invites admin or moderator, admin invites moderator only; 7-day links; re-invite cancels the old one.** (a) owner invites admin or moderator; admin invites moderator only; nobody is invited as owner; links expire after **7 days**; re-inviting the same email cancels the older invite; owner/admin may cancel or resend a pending invite (recommended) · (b) owner and admin may both invite admin or moderator · (c) only the owner invites; 48 h links (plugin default).
- **D8 People who accept an invite (M7-T6, M7-T11, M7-T14) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) `/invite/$id`; signing up from an invite creates no own workspace or trial; add a workspace switcher.** Today every sign-up gets its own workspace + billing account + trial, and the client has no workspace switcher. (a) an invite link `/invite/$invitationId` → sign up (email fixed to the invited address) **without** a first workspace, billing account or trial, or log in → accept → the invited workspace becomes active; people in 2+ workspaces get a **workspace switcher** in the top bar (recommended) · (b) same sign-up path, but someone who already belongs to a workspace can't accept (no switcher in Module 7) · (c) keep the normal sign-up (the invitee also gets an own trial workspace) + a switcher.
- **D9 What counts as a seat (M7-T5, M7-T6, M7-T13) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) seats = members incl. the owner + pending invites (Starter = owner-only).** Plans: `teamSeats` 1 | 1 | 3 | 10, pooled per account (PW-D11, `seatsLeft()` counts the owner). (a) seats = distinct members across the account's workspaces **including the owner**, **plus pending (unexpired) invites**; so Starter (1) is owner-only and Growth adds 2 people; checked at invite time and again at accept time (recommended) · (b) the owner doesn't count (seats = extra people: Starter + 1, Growth + 3, Pro + 10), pending invites count · (c) like (a) but pending invites don't count (checked only at accept).
- **D10 Over the seat limit after a downgrade or plan end (M7-T5, M7-T13) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) nobody removed after a downgrade; invites and accepts blocked with a notice.** (a) nobody is removed: new invites and accepts are refused (409 `SEAT_LIMIT_REACHED`) until the team is under the limit, and `/settings/team` shows a notice asking the owner/admin to remove members (recommended) · (b) the newest members lose access automatically until under the limit · (c) block the downgrade while over the limit.
- **D11 Owner protection and ownership transfer (M7-T6, M7-T13) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (b, with verification) exactly one owner (can't be removed, demoted or leave) + **transfer ownership** to another member: an owner with a password enters the current password twice; a Google-only owner (no password) gets a one-time code by email (`EmailService`); the new owner takes over the billing account (plan, wallet, payments) and the old owner becomes admin (rows M7-T6b, M7-T13b).** (a) exactly one owner per workspace: the owner can't be removed, demoted or leave; admin may remove/role-change moderators only, owner may change anyone else; **no ownership transfer in Module 7** (support handles it; billing account ownership would move too) (recommended) · (b) add "Transfer ownership" to an existing admin (owner becomes admin; the billing account owner moves too) · (c) allow several owners.
- **D12 Alert channels in Module 7 and plan gating (M7-T7, M7-T8, M7-T9, M7-T15) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) Email on every plan, Telegram with `messengerAlerts`, WhatsApp "Coming soon".** WhatsApp needs a WhatsApp Business Cloud API number + an approved message template (cost per message). (a) **Email** on every plan + **Telegram** with `messengerAlerts` (Growth/Pro); **WhatsApp shown as "Coming soon"** (backlog row) (recommended) · (b) Email + Telegram + WhatsApp Cloud API now (needs a business number and template approval before the gate) · (c) Email only now.
- **D13 Telegram linking (M7-T8, M7-T15) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) one platform bot with a webhook, 15-minute `t.me/<bot>?start=<code>` link, ≤ 3 chats per workspace, test-only base URL stub.** (a) one **platform bot** (`TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`, webhook `POST /api/telegram/webhook` with Telegram's secret-token header): C12 shows a one-time link `t.me/<bot>?start=<code>` (valid 15 min); the bot saves that chat (a person or a group) to the workspace; up to 3 chats per workspace, each removable; tests use a test-only `TELEGRAM_API_BASE_URL` stub (like `META_GRAPH_BASE_URL`) (recommended) · (b) each workspace pastes its own bot token + chat id (no webhook, harder for users) · (c) platform bot, but linking by typing a code to the bot that the backend reads with `getUpdates` polling (works without a public URL).
- **D14 Spike definition (M7-T7, M7-T9, M7-T15) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) rolling 60 minutes checked on each scam outcome, default threshold 10 (1–1,000), 60-minute cool-down, no quiet hours.** (a) **rolling 60 minutes**, checked when the moderation worker records a scam outcome (reading 8); alert when the count reaches the workspace's threshold (**default 10**, client sets 1–1,000); **cool-down 60 minutes** per workspace (Redis `SET NX EX`), so at most one alert an hour; no quiet hours; times shown in Dhaka time (recommended) · (b) a scheduler job every 5 minutes checks every workspace with alerts on (same window, threshold, cool-down) · (c) clock hours in Dhaka time (resets at :00), one alert per clock hour.
- **D15 Who gets alerts (M7-T7, M7-T9, M7-T15) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) members ticked on C12 (default the owner, max 10) by email + every linked Telegram chat.** (a) email to the members the owner/admin tick on C12 (default: the owner), members only, max 10; Telegram to the linked chats (recommended) · (b) every owner + admin by email, no choice · (c) a free list of up to 5 email addresses (not only members).
- **D16 Client navigation for Support and Settings (M7-T11) — ANSWERED 2026-11-20: (a) sidebar adds Support + Settings; mobile bottom bar 4 items + a "More" sheet.** The mobile bottom bar already has 7 items. (a) desktop sidebar adds **Support** and **Settings** (Settings = `/settings/team` + `/settings/alerts` as tabs); mobile bottom bar keeps 4 items (Dashboard, Comments, Rules, Analytics) + a **More** sheet with the rest (Pages, Playground, Plans, Support, Settings) (recommended) · (b) add the 2 items to both bars (9 items on mobile) · (c) Settings + Support in a user menu in the top bar only.

Built `src/email/` (`@Global() EmailModule` in `AppModule`): `EmailService.send({ to, subject, html, text, idempotencyKey? })` = one `fetch` POST `https://api.resend.com/emails` (`Authorization: Bearer`, JSON `{ from, to: [to], subject, html, text }`, optional `Idempotency-Key`, `AbortSignal.timeout` 10 s) → `{ delivered: true, id }`; errors `EmailSendError { status, retryable }` (network/timeout/429/5xx retryable; 4xx and a 2xx without id not), Resend's message capped at 300 chars with the recipient address replaced by `[recipient]`; logs = `domain=<recipient domain> id=<resend id>` / `email failed: domain status retryable`. Env `RESEND_API_KEY` (`re_…`) + `EMAIL_FROM` (`addr` or `Name <addr>`, class-validator `isEmail` `allow_display_name`): both or neither, bad values fail boot; `.env.example` + compose passthrough. Not configured: non-production logs `to` + subject and resolves `{ delivered: false, reason: 'not_configured' }`; production logs one error at boot and `send` throws `EmailNotConfiguredError` (compose runs production, so compose without a key = sends fail, callers catch). `bilingualEmail({ en, bn }, clientAppUrl)` (`src/email/bilingual-email.ts`) → `{ subject 'en · bn' one line, html (inline styles, `lang` blocks, everything escaped), text }`; CTA must have CLIENT_APP_URL's origin and base path (`EmailLinkError`). Test fake `createEmailServiceFake()` (`test/utils/fake-email.ts`: `expectSends(n)`, `failNext(err)`, `sent`, `unexpected`, `pending`, `reset`; unarmed send throws). Choices: no `RESEND_API_BASE_URL` test override (D17 uses the fake in backend e2e); dev log omits the body (invite links / OTPs are secrets). |
